'I reckon:' Liverpool verdict on 'physically dominant' sensation revealed

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Liverpool have a lot of decisions to make before the end of the 2026-27 season.

Right now, they don't really look like they're going to be able to compete for the Premier League title or the Champions League.

Indeed, there are only seven points between them and first-placed Arsenal but, considering the Reds have already been beaten by Mikel Arteta's men once this season, that gap is going to be hard to break down.

Obviously, if they end up drawing or losing a couple of games like they did against Newcastle on Saturday, they could be in with a chance but, if it ends up being down to Liverpool, fans wouldn't really hold out much hope.

That is, especially since they were beaten 3-2 in the Champions League by Fenerbahce.

Changes are clearly needed at Anfield which is why the upcoming transfer windows are going to be massive for them.

Liverpool would have to slash Ronald Araujo's buy clause in half to sign him permanently

Nobody knows what Liverpool are going to do in the winter or summer transfer windows.

They have thrown their hat into many different rings with almost every position among their ranks covered by a new target or two.

Some players that the Reds have been linked with have been exciting for fans but others, not so much.

There are also some decisions to make about their current crop of players with Ronald Araujo's future being one of the most talked-about deals in the world.

Within their season-long loan deal, Liverpool have a €55 million option to buy the Uruguayan and, even though he's performed rather well, the Reds are still undecided about whether they want to activate that option to buy or not.

And, now, thanks to an update from David Lynch via Anfield Index, it's now clear what Liverpool's plans are.

"That figure [€55 million] is far too high for Liverpool to be interested," he said.

"You’re talking it would have to be, I reckon, about even half that much to even give a sniff of Liverpool being interested."

Liverpool need to make their decision on Ronald Araujo soon

Although there is still a long while until June comes around, a decision on Araujo needs to be made.

He's currently Liverpool's first-choice right-back and, without him among their ranks, even more problems would be coming the Reds' way.

Obviously, his prior experience of La Liga and the Champions League has massively helped him adjust to Premier League life but, since he's 27 years old and has a rather ropey injury history, it feels like a lot of money for Liverpool to commit to.

That is, especially when they don't know how good Conor Bradley is going to be upon his return from injury.

Everything is up in the air at then moment but, even if they don't want to, a decision is going to have to be made soon.

Otherwise, the Reds could end up missing out on the "physically dominant" defender or going for someone who's worse than the Uruguayan which would be far from ideal.

Guide: GTA 6: Secret Controls the Game Doesn't Tell You

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

GTA 6 has a complicated control scheme that allows you to interact with the game in a variety of different ways. And while Rockstar does a good job introducing almost all of the title's mechanics, several useful features and commands are never mentioned at all. In this GTA 6 guide, we're going to tell you about some of its secret controls.

On this page: Best Secret Controls GTA 6 Doesn't Tell You

Below are 12 secret controls in GTA 6 the game doesn't tell you about.

Please note, these all apply to the Default control scheme, which you can enable by pressing Options > Settings > Control Scheme.

Double tap Triangle to get out of your car while the engine's still running:

This can be useful for when you need to make a swift getaway, as you want waste time needing to start the engine.

One other quick tip here: if you're with a partner and need to quickly get into the back seat, hold Triangle so your character doesn't waste time running around to the passenger side.

If the police know your identity but not necessarily your car, one way you can avoid being detected is by hiding behind the wheel.

Just park up somewhere discreet, and hold Circle to hide from any pursuing police:

You can rest and lean on any surface by standing close to it and holding the Circle button, like shown:

This can be useful if you're casing a joint, or just want to watch the world pass you by.

You don't always need words to get your point across. There are situations where a middle finger will do.

Fortunately, GTA 6 has a solution for scenarios just like that.

While on foot, hold L2 and press R1 to give someone the finger:

You can also do this while driving by simply pressing R1 while behind the wheel:

Tapping Left and Right while driving will turn on your indicators, allowing you to signal to other motorists which way you're going:

This can actually be useful when you're trying to drive safely, as oncoming cars will understand which way you're going and react accordingly. It can also help when you're merging lanes on motorways.

While more complex radio controls can be accessed by holding Left, did you know you can also quickly turn the radio on and off by double-tapping Left?

This works both in a car and when you're out and about using your earbuds.

If you press Square while driving, you can quickly attack with your car door, as shown:

This is really useful when you're being flanked by unsuspecting bikers — or you just want to show cyclists who's king the road!

Much like the above car door attack, you can kick any nearby enemies by pushing Square while you're riding a bike, as shown:

This can help you knock rival motorcylists off their bike, or just give you a good core workout while on the highway.

You can go first-person at any time while you're on-foot and stationary by holding L2 and pressing R3:

You can move the camera around from this position and press R2 to zoom, but you do need to hold L2 down throughout.

It's also worth noting you can't move while doing this, as there's no traditional first-person mode in the game.

This can be useful for reading signs and other looking closely at other objects in the game, though.

Every vehicle has a full suite of options you can access by holding Left and using the right analogue stick to navigate up to Vehicle, as shown:

These options can include everything from winding down your windows to turning on your headlights. Speaking of which, you can also toggle your headlights at any time by pressing Circle:

And, if you're in a boat, you can use the above vehicle management options to set down an anchor, which will keep it in place while you go diving:

If you're playing on the PS5, the DualSense controller's adaptive triggers will help you observe the speed limit.

While you're driving, holding R2 until you feel the first point of resistance. This is the speed limit.

You'll need to have Adaptive Triggers Resistance enabled to feel this, and you can check it's on by pressing Options > Settings > Controller > DualSense Wireless Controller Features:

You can take two cars when you're with your partner by using the command menu.

Hold Right to bring up the menu and press Cross/X to instruct your partner to drive:

Then get in another car and hold Right again then press Square to follow you:

You'll notice that your partner will drive right behind you, which can be useful if you need to setup getaway cars during robberies:

Did you know about any or all of these secret controls in GTA 6? You can find even more tips and tricks through the link, as well as tons of collectible and mission walkthroughs in our GTA 6 guide.

Poch denied first Man Utd win by Lammens howler at Sporting

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Sporting Lisbon vs Manchester United. UEFA Champions League Round 1.

Report as Man Utd self-destruct in bizarre Champions League draw at Sporting after having two goals controversially ruled out; Bruno Fernandes and Luis Suarez both scored two goals, one penalty each, with the Man Utd captain's second goal freakishly bouncing in off a divot in the ground

Please use Chrome browser for a more accessible video player

Mauricio Pochettino was denied a first victory as Manchester United head coach by a goalkeeping howler from Senne Lammens in an incident-packed 2-2 draw with Sporting in the Champions League as Youri Tielemans was sent off late on.

United looked to be on course for a first away win of the season after captain Bruno Fernandes scored two goals in the first five minutes of the second half only for Lammens to spill the ball in the box for Luis Suarez to equalise with his second goal of the game.

Pochettino thought United had gone back in front for a second time shortly after Lammens' error when Sporting goalkeeper Kaique punched into his own net at a corner. However, it was ruled out following an on-field review for a foul by substitute Patrick Dorgu.

It was a damaging night for Lammens, who gave away the penalty for Suarez's first goal just 75 seconds after saving the Sporting striker's poorly taken spot-kick. The man brought into replace the error-prone Andre Onana is now beginning to resemble his predecessor.

Fernandes' equaliser three minutes after the restart came from the third penalty of what was a bizarre match. Few moments topped the United skipper's freakish second goal which came thanks a tame effort bouncing up off a divot in the ground and into back of the net.

Tielemans' stoppage-time red will only add to United's frustrations. It was his first booking, for a tame follow-through on Goncalo Inacio, that denied Amad Diallo from equalising late in the first half. "It is a terrible decision," said Sky Sports pundit Clinton Morrison.

Pochettino's first two matches have seen his side have two players sent off and concede two penalties. If he did not know it before, he certainly knows now that nothing comes easy at United, who sit 19th in the Champions League and Premier League.

Sky Sports pundit Jamie Carragher to CBS:

"I do think United were a little bit unlucky tonight with the goal disallowed just before half-time. The goal at the end felt very harsh to me, the one where it could have been 3-2...

"The focus on a Man Utd goalkeeper is always big if he's making mistakes. The back-ups aren't even at the level where you could say no matter how poorly the goalkeeper plays, you could change it. You worry about the confidence of that goalkeeper now.

"That mistake for the equaliser, the 2-2 goal, you say sometimes 'an amateur goalkeeper wouldn't make that mistake' and that just can't happen at this level."

Man Utd head coach Mauricio Pochettino to TNT Sports:

"I'm so proud of the players. It was a tough game. I liked the team we played for 90 minutes, the personality, the character. It's difficult to find a game where you get punished in one game. We were punished a lot in key actions. Today we showed how we want to play after just one week of working together.

"We need to concentrate and focus more, try to recover better. The performance like today's is the best medicine to prepare for the next game."

Ref Watch: Three Szymon Marciniak blunders in Sporting vs Man United

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Correspondent AUTHORITY Football journalist with experience at Reach PLC and PA Media; University of Central Lancashire graduate. FOCUS Fan-engagement content and match analysis across the Premier League and Championship. THE INSIGHT Oliver utilises a network of club and industry contacts to deliver verified reporting and fan-first content. He provides the human stories behind the results to ensure fans get the full picture as it happens.

Szymon Marciniak was the centre of attention as Man United were held to a 2-2 draw by Sporting CP in the Champions League.

Mauricio Pochettino’s side conceded an early penalty as Lisandro Martinez clipped Geny Catamo, but Senne Lammens saved Luis Suarez’s weak spot-kick.

Lammens then brought down Maxi Araujo in the box just over a minute later, and Suarez made no mistake with his second penalty.

Bruno Fernandes equalised after the break from the spot and then put the visitors ahead soon after, but Suarez bagged his brace late on.

Youri Tielemans was sent off in the closing stages as Marciniak refereed in a completely bizarre manner throughout.

Martinez was left dumbfounded when Marciniak pointed to the spot in the 29th minute following his challenge on Catamo.

It certainly looked contentious and he went down easily, and replays showed that there was minimal contact and the Sporting winger was searching for a foul to then go down.

The Polish official bought his theatrics and gave Sporting a penalty, so it was just as well that Lammens saved it.

One of the worst decisions seen in a long time came five minutes before the break as Tielemans was adjudged to have fouled Goncalo Inacio before Amad levelled the scoreline.

The Belgian midfielder slid in but did not even touch his opponent as he played Amad in before he eventually forced the ball home on the rebound.

Marciniak incredibly blew his whistle for a foul just moments before the ball crossed the goal line, meaning VAR could not get involved.

It was never a foul and should have stood, with United rightly feeling robbed and Tielemans booked for a foul that never was.

United's bad luck continued as they were robbed of a late winner by Marciniak and his officials.

Fernandes' corner was met by Sporting goalkeeper Pereira as he diverted the ball into his own net but quickly complained of being fouled.

None of the Red Devils players did anything to impede him, yet the referee reviewed the goal and incredibly ruled it out.

Steelers players react to Aaron Rodgers' release as some teammates back QB amid Mike McCarthy controversy

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

If not listed, please contact your TV provider.

While Aaron Rodgers was publicly scorching Mike McCarthy, several Steelers watched their now-former teammate inside the locker room following Wednesday's practice. It was a surreal moment in a truly bizarre 24 hours for the Steelers, who ultimately released Rodgers after his criticism of McCarthy.

Pittsburgh announced late Tuesday night that Rodgers would be inactive against the Broncos and would be replaced by Mason Rudolph. Rodgers, during an appearance on The Pat McAfee Show, accused McCarthy of being late and sleeping through meetings, not watching film of opponents the week of games and saying, "1-2-3 Packers!" in the locker room at halftime during Sunday's eventual loss to the Eagles. McCarthy has since confirmed his Packers snafu in a press conference, calling it an embarrassing moment.

Anonymous team sources corroborated Rodgers' claim that McCarthy fell asleep in meetings, according to The Athletic.

"He's not lying," an unnamed player said.

Several players were interviewed at their locker, including tight end Pat Freiermuth, rookie quarterback Drew Allar, rookie receiver Germie Bernard, and veteran wideout Marquez Valdes-Scantling, who was also Rodgers' teammate in Green Bay. Each spoke favorably about Rodgers.

"I think he's the best player to ever play this game as his position," Valdes-Scantling said.

"I wouldn't be where I am today without him." MVS reacts to Aaron Rodgers after his appearance on McAfee pic.twitter.com/pYN9KZMqrB

Rodgers said that Freiermuth informed him Tuesday night of the Steelers' decision to deactivate him.

"I was just checking in on a close friend," Freiermuth said.

Pat Freiermuth speaks on McCarthy and Rodgers pic.twitter.com/wtjmKuE6hr

Allar started to address questions about Rodgers but stopped after he was waived off by veteran safety DeShon Elliott.

"For me, it's kinda sad to see, because I have a lot of respect for Aaron," Allar said. "I've learned a lot from him since he's been here. I think I'm gonna leave it at that and just with Aaron the best."

Drew Allar just now: #Steelers pic.twitter.com/0hUsgnCnGE

Bernard shared a message that McCarthy told the team earlier in the day.

"Just gotta come together. It's all about unity," he said. "Coach told us today that the best teams are player-led, and we have all the veterans, we have all the talent in the world to do that. So we just have to lean on each other."

Bernard also exuded confidence in Rudolph, who will make his first start of the season Friday against the Broncos.

"Mason is a great quarterback," he said. "We have complete trust in him, and he has complete trust in us. We've just got to go out there and believe in what he can do so we can get it done."

UCL | PSG 2-2 Roma: Giallorossi hold reigning Champions

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Roma earned a more than creditable 2-2 draw away to reigning Champions League holders PSG, as Mario Hermoso and Donyell Malen’s penalty cancelled out Illia Zabarnyi and Ousmane Dembélé.

The Giallorossi had twice thrown away two-goal leads for defeat to Real Madrid and Manchester United, but at least the injury crisis was calming down, with only Devyne Rensch and Lorenzo Pellegrini still out.

Surprisingly, Donyell Malen, Paulo Dybala and Bryan Cristante were initially benched for Niccolò Pisilli and Santiago Castro. Former Roma coach Luis Enrique lost Khvicha Kvaratskhelia and Désiré Doué to torn anterior cruciate ligaments in the space of a few weeks.

It was Roma who took the lead after just five minutes when Mario Hermoso’s high press saw him rob Maghnes Akliouche, then Matias Soulé dribbled down the left and just about kept it in play to pull back from the by-line, where Hermoso was ready to fire in.

Mario Hermoso opens the scoring against PSG!

📺 Stream now on TNT Sports & HBO Max https://t.co/fRCRh6tXYB https://t.co/pvnFFUqgg5

— Football on TNT Sports (@footballontnt) November 25, 2026

Achraf Hakimi needed a well-timed header to take the Soulé cross off the head of Castro at the back post, while Nuno Mendes failed to trouble Mile Svilar.

The biggest problem for Gian Piero Gasperini’s side this season has been corner kicks, and they were punished yet again by an Illia Zabarnyi free header at the near post with very little marking on the Senny Mayulu set play.

Evan N’Dicka also threatened from a corner with a header flashing inches wide, then after the restart Safonov flapped a Wesley cross into the path of Hermoso to volley just over the bar.

Roma had a huge opportunity to restore their lead on 51 minutes when Pisilli ran from midfield and combined with Castro to force a desperate one-handed Safonov save.

Joao Neves was perhaps offside anyway when his diving header went wide on the Nuno Mendes cross, but Wesley was caught out by the pace of ex-Inter full-back Achraf Hakimi, pulling him back by the shoulder for a penalty. PSG also wanted a red card, but the referee opted for the yellow. Ousmane Dembele just came on and converted the penalty.

Malen and Dybala came off the bench, and it was La Joya’s lay-off for Soulé that saw the shot charged down in the six-yard box by the raised arm of Vitinha, eventually spotted by VAR for a penalty, converted by Malen.

It remained wide open to the end, as first Hermoso turned over from a very promising position when off balance, then Fabian Ruiz had two attempts charged down, as Hakimi drilling inches wide in added time.

Dembele forced a difficult Svilar save with the boot at the near post, but it was a point apiece in Paris.

Mike McCarthy confirms he said "1-2-3 Packers" at halftime last Sunday

📅 2026-11-25  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Aaron Rodgers said a number of things that caught the attention of the football world on Wednesday and one of the comments that drew a big response had to do with something that happened in the locker room at halftime of last Sunday’s loss to the Eagles.

After saying head coach Mike McCarthy fell asleep in meetings and missed other ones altogether in his first comments since being benched on Tuesday night, Rodgers said that McCarthy broke down the team in the locker room with “1-2-3 Packers” in reference to the team he coached through the 2018 season. In a press conference later in the day, McCarthy confirmed that happened but said he took issue with how Rodgers characterized other things that included his commitment to the job.

“The Philadelphia situation at halftime was very emotional. . . . Embarrassing moment, but I just think we work a lot of hours so definitely a difference of opinion from what I was told about the comments,” McCarthy said.

The Steelers have released Rodgers and McCarthy stressed the need for the team to focus on Friday’s game against the Broncos, although it feels like that’s going to be difficult based on how the last 18 hours have played out in Pittsburgh.

A Look at All the ‘Dancing With the Stars’ Winners Over the Years—Including Our Latest Champions

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Mirrorball Trophy > Olympic/Paralympic Gold Medal.

At this point, Dancing With the Stars has been on for a frightening amount of time—meaning a truly shocking number of celebrities have wrapped themselves in stretchy glitter onesies and pirouetted their way around that hallowed ballroom while regretting all their life choices, deep in contemplation about firing their agent. In fact, 35 stars have already taken home the coveted Mirrorball Trophy—ranging from people only your mom could pick out of an early ’90s lineup to some pretty famous faces, including several Olympians/Paralympians and Bachelor/Bachelorette stars.

In honor of DWTS season 35 coming to a close (no, I’m not okay, but thanks for asking), let’s take a look back at allllllllll the Dancing With the Stars winners throughout history.

Runners-up: John O’Hurley & Charlotte Jørgensen

About Kelly: She’s a model and actress best known for playing Sam McCall on General Hospital. For more info, ask my grandma, who’s been watching that soap for years.

Runners-up: Jerry Rice & Anna Trebunskaya

About Drew: This dude is Nick Lachey’s younger brother who was also in 98 Degrees. You just fully forgot he existed, it’s fine.

Runners-up: Mario Lopez & Karina Smirnoff

About Emmitt: He’s a running back who played for the Dallas Cowboys! But obviously, this DWTS win is also important.

About Apolo: He’s a not-at-all casual eight-time Olympic track speed skating medalist. I mean...his talent.

About Hélio: He’s a Brazilian race car driver who won the Indy 500 three full times, no bigs.

Runners-up: Jason Taylor & Edyta Śliwińska

About Kristi: She’s just casually one of the most famous Olympic figure skaters ever. I’m sure this Mirrorball Trophy will look great next to all her medals!

About Brooke: Ironically, you probably know Brooke best from cohosting DWTS Seasons 10 to 17, but she’s also an actress!

About Shawn: Just another casual Olympian! Shawn is a 2008 Olympic balance beam gold medalist and also the 2007 all-around world champion.

About Donny: Meet the man who recorded “I’ll Make a Man Out of You” from Mulan, otherwise known as the most famous member of sibling group The Osmonds.

Runners-up: Evan Lysacek & Anna Trebunskaya

About Nicole: I mean...she is only THE lead singer of The Pussycat Dolls. Need I say more?

Runners-up: Kyle Massey & Lacey Schwimmer

About Jennifer: To me, she’ll always be the sister from Ferris Bueller’s Day Off, aka my personal hero.

Runners-up: Kirstie Alley & Maksim Chmerkovskiy

About Hines: A football coach, former wide receiver, and Super Bowl MVP, Hines has also had cameos in The Dark Knight Rises and The Walking Dead.

Runners-up: Rob Kardashian & Cheryl Burke

About J.R.: An actor, veteran, and motivational speaker, J.R. has appeared on All My Children and really knows how to pull off a pair of suspenders.

Runners-up: Katherine Jenkins & Mark Ballas

About Donald: He played for the Green Bay Packers and helped them win Super Bowl XLV! Again, almost as impressive as the Mirrorball Trophy. Almost.

About Melissa: Um, she won The Bachelor only to have Jason Mesnick break up with her for Molly Maleney in what might actually have been the most dramatic season ever.

Runners-up: Zendaya & Valentin Chmerkovskiy

About Kellie: If she looks familiar, that is because Kellie was a star on American Idol and has since had a pretty successful country career!

Runners-up: Corbin Bleu & Karina Smirnoff

About Amber: Not only was she in Glee, duh, she’s a Grammy-nominated singer and starred in the Dreamgirls musical.

About Meryl: You guessed it, she’s yet another Olympic ice dancer who happens to be just as talented off her skates.

Runners-up: Sadie Robertson & Mark Ballas

About Alfonso: Not only does he host Dancing With the Stars and America’s Funniest Home Videos, he played Carlton on The Fresh Prince of Bel-Air!

About Rumer: She’s well-known for being Bruce Willis and Demi Moore’s daughter, but I stan Rumer for her movies—nay, films—The House Bunny and Sorority Row.

Prediction: Dinamo Zagreb vs Bayer Leverkusen

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Competition: UEFA Europa League Date: Thursday, November 26, 2026 Kickoff: 17:45 GMT / 18:45 CET Venue: Stadion Maksimir, Zagreb

Verdict: Bayer Leverkusen to win Best odds: 73/100 Bookmaker: 1xBet

Jerko Leko has enjoyed a perfect start in the Dinamo Zagreb dugout, but Thursday brings a demanding examination of that early progress. Bayer Leverkusen visits Maksimir after a convincing Bundesliga win, with just two points separating the clubs in the Europa League standings ahead of their first competitive meeting.

Three games, three wins and eight goals have changed the mood around Dinamo. Leko began with a 2-0 victory at Sunderland, followed it with a 3-1 success at Varazdin and then marked his home debut with Sunday’s 3-2 win over Istra 1961.

The club’s report of the Istra match described a performance that could have produced a wider winning margin. Luka Stojkovic, Fran Topic and Dani Rodriguez shared the goals, giving Dinamo several reasons to feel encouraged about its attacking play.

Dion Beljo remains the central threat. The striker has scored 13 league goals and four in the Europa League this season, making him an obvious concern for the visiting defense. Dinamo also has three clean sheets from four European games, although the 4-2 home defeat to NEC Nijmegen showed how quickly that defensive record can unravel.

One detail from Leverkusen’s opponent analysis deserves attention: Dinamo has allowed 57 opposition attempts in the competition, compared with the German side’s 29. Keeping the visitor away from shooting positions will require a considerable improvement on that underlying record.

Scott McKenna and Robert Mudrazija remain unavailable. Niko Galesic and Sergi Dominguez are options in central defense, while Beljo is expected to lead the attack. Stojkovic’s productive weekend strengthens his case for another prominent role behind the striker.

Leverkusen’s 4-0 victory over Paderborn supplied a welcome reminder of its attacking quality. Afonso Moreira scored twice, Patrik Schick and Ezequiel Fernandez also found the net, and Nathan Tella marked his return from injury with two assists.

Schick has now scored nine times in the opening ten Bundesliga matchdays. Moreira’s contribution adds another threat around him, while Malik Tillman also helped create the opening goal against Paderborn. For Carles Martinez Novell, the challenge is to reproduce that fluency against an opponent with far more confidence than it had a month ago.

The Europa League campaign began with three victories and three clean sheets, including a 4-0 win at Lech Poznan. A 1-0 home defeat to Marseille interrupted that sequence, leaving Leverkusen on nine points. A win in Zagreb would restore momentum and create a useful cushion over a nearby rival.

Martin Terrier and Kennet Eichhorn remain absent. Victor Boniface and Tim Oermann are not registered for the Europa League, despite Oermann returning to domestic action against Paderborn. Tella’s successful comeback gives Martinez another attacking option, with Schick expected to start up front.

Dinamo’s revival and Beljo’s form should make this a difficult away assignment. Our preference is still Leverkusen, whose improving attacking options look capable of exploiting the chances the host has allowed in Europe. We expect Dinamo to score, with the visitor edging an entertaining contest 2-1.

Verdict: Bayer Leverkusen to win Best odds: 73/100 Bookmaker: 1xBet

Prediction: Crystal Palace vs Real Sociedad

The four new games added to PlayStation Plus on November 24, 2026, are:

All four titles are available on PlayStation 5, but Like a Dragon: Pirate Yakuza in Hawaii is available to users on PS4 as well. The interesting Mason Rudolph comments that came right before Aaron Rodgers’ shock benching

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Mike McCarthy may have tipped his hand more than a week before he moved on from Aaron Rodgers in an absolute stunner.

Longtime NFL reporter Charles Robinson re-posted a clip after the news broke of Rodgers' benching of Mike Tirico and Cris Collinsworth discussing backup Mason Rudolph after he replaced Rodgers at the end of the team's blowout "Sunday Night Football" loss to the Bengals on Nov. 15.

"Kind of an interesting deal in Pittsburgh as we talked to Mike McCarthy last night," Tirico said late in the fourth quarter. "They're a roster with four quarterbacks, people were looking for people at the trade deadline. No real movement of the quarterbacks here. Aaron says it's his last year, Mason Rudolph has been around the Steelers facility for quite some time, Will Howard, Drew Allar, Penn Stater, drafted. Mike McCarthy said, 'I've got some pretty good quarterbacks here.'"

Collinsworth followed: "He even brought up the idea that hey, everybody assumes we're going to take a quarterback with Aaron retiring. We've got pretty good quarterbacks in here, as if, 'I don't know about that. I want one of these young quarterbacks or do I want to take one of these guys that we've had a full year to develop here and let them play?' If it falls to them in the right way, they probably will. If not, I think they just roll out Mason Rudolph. They've been impressed."

Robinson initially posted the clip stating that it shows why teams should not let coaches dictate teams' quarterback plans and why he believes McCarthy said something of note in the production meeting, citing that the 31-year-old Rudolph is not a viable long-term option.

Tuesday's news made that segment raise even more eyebrows.

"Absolutely time to revisit this #Steelers clip. Given what just happened with Rodgers, I refuse to believe Mike McCarthy didn't say something in the production meeting," he tweeted.

"There's no way. The Rudolph stuff is wildly on the nose of where we are right now with Rodgers getting benched."

Production meetings happen between coaches and announcers before games, providing a mix of on- and off-the-record details to help the broadcasters prepare for the game.

That Tirico and Collinsworth shared the Rudolph discussion indicated that McCarthy did not mind it getting out there, and it's certainly notable that Rudolph is now starting Friday.

Mason Rudolph entering the Week 10 loss. @CharlesRobinson/X

This could potentially be a seven-game audition for Rudolph — and possibly Howard and/or Allar — to show they should receive the chance to start next season versus drafting a quarterback early.

The Steelers always knew this would be Rodgers' last season and this upcoming quarterback draft class has depth, although there are questions about how good some of the options are.

Presumed first-rounders like Arch Manning and Dante Moore have only seen their stock fall, and the Steelers currently own the 21st pick in the 2027 NFL Draft.

Pittsburgh Steelers quarterback Aaron Rodgers (8) scrambles during an NFL football game against the Philadelphia Eagles, Sunday, Nov. 22, 2026, in Philadelphia. AP Photo/Chris Szagola

As Robinson noted, the book is probably out on Rudolph at this point considering his age and that Friday will be just his 20th start since being drafted in 2018.

It's hard to envision a team with contending ambitions like the Steelers would truly believe he gives them their best chance to win in 2027.

There are also questions about the viability of Howard, a 2025 sixth-rounder, but Allar provides a potentially intriguing option.

Pittsburgh Steelers head coach Mike McCarthy speaks during a news conference after an NFL football game in Philadelphia, Sunday, Nov. 22, 2026. AP Photo/Chris Szagola

The former Nittany Lions star had been projected as a potential first-rounder in the 2024 Draft, but he instead returned to Happy Valley.

That decision proved disastrous with his play declining before he suffered a season-ending injury, and Allar ultimately fell to the third round the 76th-overall pick.

One decision the Steelers (6-4) will have to weigh, should Rodgers be completely done, is whether they want to see Allar or Howard this season considering they are the No. 7 seed in the playoffs.

Mikel Arteta lauded Viktor Gyokeres' performance against Borussia Dortmund, stating "that's the Viktor we needed".

Arsenal were held to a 1-1 draw by Dortmund in the Champions League on Tuesday, with Maximilian Beier cancelling out Gyokeres' stunning opener.

The Sweden international recorded 50 touches, his most in a Premier League or Champions League game for Arsenal, while also winning six of his 14 duels.

Gyokeres has scored in back-to-back Champions League starts for just the second time, after also doing so for Sporting CP in October-November 2024.

And only Erling Haaland (27) and Joao Pedro (18) have more goals among Premier League players in 2026 than Gyokeres (17), who earned high praise from his manager.

"I think he had a brilliant performance," Arteta said of Gyokeres.

"I think he scored an unbelievable goal. He has the technique and the quality to do that.

"What I love the most is how connected he was to the team. We used him many times in different ways.

"His link-up play, the way he held the ball, the way he threaded in behind and stretched the pitch. There was a lot to like in that performance.

Gyokeres told Sky Sports: "It's been a good last couple of games for me.

"Obviously, we probably should've won [against Dortmund] with the amount of chances we had.

"Overall, I think we played really well, that was good at least. In one way it's a bit frustrating but the way we attacked and the amount of chances we created, it's just a matter of time until there's going to be a lot of goals."

Arsenal's 30 shots against Dortmund are their most on record in a Champions League game (since 2003-04), and the Gunners' most in a game that they failed to win across all competitions since December 2023 vs West Ham– also 30.

French team: Zinédine Zidane reveals the plan with Kylian Mbappé

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Beaten by Italy (3-1) this Thursday evening in the Nations League, the French team will have to quickly raise their heads. Zinédine Zidane’s men will return to Turkey on Sunday with, perhaps, the return of Kylian Mbappé. Absent against the Squadra Azzurra due to an injury, the captain of the Blues remained with the group in the hope of being able to play this second meeting.

Asked at the microphone of TF1 after the defeat against the Italians, Zinédine Zidane was rather optimistic regarding a return of his attacker. “If he is going to play against Turkey? I hope, of course. He stayed for that. I don’t think it’s a big deal (his injury, editor’s note). We’ll see tomorrow, but that’s the idea.”explained the French coach. Good news in perspective for the Blues.

Aston Villa winger Alejandro Garnacho has aimed a subtle dig at the club by removing all mention of them from his Instagram.

The Argentine, currently on loan at Villa from Chelsea, has endured a difficult season so far, having found himself far down in Unai Emery’s pecking order.

Garnacho has had a difficult few years in general, having fallen out of favour and earned a poor reputation at Manchester United before being sold to Chelsea.

Things didn’t go much better for Garnacho at Stamford Bridge either, leading to him being loaned out to Villa this season, though he’s once again quickly found himself out of favour.

See below for Garnacho’s Instagram page, in which he’s removed the profile picture and deleted most of his recent photos…

Garnacho may well have given up on trying to win his place back in Emery’s squad, and that’s not a great look for the player, even if this must have been a frustrating season for him so far.

The 22-year-old looked like a big prospect a few years ago, but his career has quickly gone downhill and he really needs to show he can improve his attitude and professionalism.

It’s not clear why Garnacho has so quickly struggled to establish himself at Villa, but it’s not a great sign, and it raises questions about what kind of character he is.

Both Man Utd and Chelsea got fed up of Garnacho eventually, and with Villa it’s happened even more quickly.

There’s a talented player in there somewhere, but it’s not clear if he’s being badly advised by someone close to him, or if perhaps there’s his side to the story which we haven’t heard yet.

Prediction: Inter vs Stuttgart

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Competition: UEFA Champions League Date: Wednesday, November 25, 2026 Kickoff: 20:00 GMT / 21:00 CET Venue: San Siro, Milan

Verdict: Inter to win Best odds: 11/25 Bookmaker: 1xBet

Inter has little time to dwell on a remarkable evening in Bergamo. After surrendering a two-goal advantage late in Sunday’s 4-4 draw with Atalanta, Cristian Chivu’s side returns to San Siro with another important European assignment. Stuttgart arrives a point ahead of the host in the Champions League standings, making this a useful opportunity for Inter to improve its position.

Four goals should usually be enough to win away from home. Inter discovered otherwise at Atalanta, where Pio Esposito’s hat-trick was followed by a Petar Sucic penalty that made it 4-2. Two late goals left the Italian champion with a single point and gave Chivu plenty to address before Wednesday.

There was encouragement in the attacking performance. Marcus Thuram repeatedly unsettled the home defense, Esposito finished with confidence and Lautaro Martinez returned from injury. Chivu has several credible combinations available up front, a useful advantage when a European game becomes stretched late on.

Inter’s continental campaign has also taken a healthier direction. Defeats against Real Madrid and Club Brugge were followed by a 1-0 victory over Shakhtar Donetsk and a 3-0 win at Feyenoord. Those consecutive clean sheets offer a reminder that the team can manage games far more effectively than it did on Sunday.

Hakan Calhanoglu and Piotr Zielinski are unavailable, with Chivu confirming his midfield limitations before the match. Ange-Yoan Bonny is also sidelined. John Stones is available, while Alessandro Bastoni returns from his Champions League suspension. Lautaro’s comeback gives the coach another option alongside Thuram and Esposito.

Stuttgart made an excellent start to the league phase, beating Viking 3-1 and Slovan Bratislava 3-0. The subsequent fixtures have been less rewarding: a 1-1 draw with Atletico Madrid was followed by a 2-0 defeat at Galatasaray, leaving Sebastian Hoeness’ side on seven points from four games.

The Bundesliga picture has been uneven. A goalless draw with Schalke at the weekend left Stuttgart with four wins and five defeats from ten league matches. Its European performances deserve respect, although the trip to Milan demands a level of concentration that has been difficult to maintain domestically.

Stuttgart should still have opportunities. Inter’s missing midfielders could make the first pass out of defense less reliable, and an aggressive visiting press would test those replacements. The danger for Stuttgart comes when that pressure is bypassed, allowing Inter’s forwards to attack space.

Stuttgart has confirmed that every player registered for the Champions League is available. Angelo Stiller and Luca Jaquez return to the squad after injury, improving Hoeness’ options in midfield and defense.

Inter’s midfield absences and Sunday’s defensive lapses leave Stuttgart a route into the game. Even so, the home side’s attacking options and recent European recovery make it our choice. We expect a closer contest than the short match odds might suggest, with Inter doing enough to win 2-1.

Verdict: Inter to win Best odds: 11/25 Bookmaker: 1xBet

Prediction: Crystal Palace vs Real Sociedad

How much better is the Texans offense than last season?

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Thursday night games throw off the schedule a little. The rearview mirror feature comes on the previous Friday which opens up a hole in the Value of Things week. However, these holes provide us with a unique opportunity to finish a thought we started on Monday. The big question was whether the offense or defense was more responsible for the team's struggles in the early going?

As you might imagine, there is no single answer to that question. These answers come in a game by game basis and the basic numbers say something different than the more advanced analytics. We will look at both in this go around. The general idea is to look at the basic numbers on a game by game and aggregate basis. Those would include points, total yards, passing yards, rushing yards, and turnovers. Once we have established ourselves there we can move onto the more possession by possession statistics that we looked at for the defense last time around. Let's start with the basics.

As in most things, this becomes a good news/bad news kind of deal. The good news is that the team in general is much better at putting points on the board than they were a year ago. The Texans set the record for field goals in 2025. They aren't going to get there this season at their current pace. They have scored far more touchdowns than they did a season ago. So, their points per game average is higher and is in the top ten in the league in that department.

They are generally gaining yards as well and limiting turnovers. So, in the grand scheme of things, when you are scoring points, gaining yards, and limiting turnovers it seems foolish to suggest that the offense is the problem. This is the natural difference when we view the world through the aggregate and when we view it at game level. Like with the defense, the offense has coasted on several huge offensive games to make the whole seem greater than the sum of the parts. Six of the ten games have seen 350 or more total yards and four of the ten games have seen 30 or more points. Let's see what happens when we break it down by possession.

A couple of points come forward that should be explored. The Texans have surrendered points on a higher percentage of possessions than they have scored. Yet. they have scored more points than they have surrendered. That means that their defense is doing a better job of holding their opponents to field goals. The offense is also doing a good job of putting the ball in the end zone. So, no set of numbers is going to be all good or all bad. It is always going to cut both ways.

On four occasions, the Texans offense has scored on more than half of their possessions. Compare that with the defense that allowed a 50 percent rate or higher four times. What is interesting is that the Texans are 3-1 in games where they scored on 50 percent or more times. They were 0-4 when they allowed a 50 percent or higher rate. They are sixth in the NFL with 42 points. So, while they haven't turned it over when you combine their punts with the fact that they are 31st in fourth down conversions.

So, the Texans offense is a tale of two teams. On the one hand, they are scoring more points and gaining more yards than they did a season ago. On the other hand, there are definite situations where they get bogged down and those situations have contributed to the problems on defense. Bill O'Brien always talked about complimentary football. It got to be annoying after awhile, but he wasn't wrong. When you punt more often and go three and out in particular you are putting more pressure on your defense. We have seen this play out often in the last two years. It would be instructive to look at the basic and more advanced numbers from the last two seasons along with the situational ones.

Is this a better offense? On balance you would have to say so based on these numbers. They are scoring more points, gaining more yards, and turning the ball over fewer times. What's not to like? Obviously, the rushing attack is the one thing that is not to like. There is more than one way to look at those numbers though. We could say the running attack is worse or we could say they are leaning into the passing attack more. That by itself would be a sign of progress.

Still, are they scoring on the same number of a possessions. This time we are altering the numbers slightly to include missed field goals in the failed possessions. It will also change the number of yards per possession. Let's see what happens when we break things down on a per possession basis. Are the Texans actually any better than they were a season ago?

So, the 2026 Texans are slightly better, but some would consider the difference to be statistical noise. So, the main difference in this year's team is simply that they are better at scoring touchdowns. That's not nothing, but when the final epitaph on this season and this offense is told, we should report the facts and give them the weight they deserve. This is a better offense, but it might be tough to call it a good one all things considered.

Raheem Sterling's chaotic descent into insanity is a product of the cocooned football world where stars can't handle rejection, writes IAN HERBERT - now he must listen to those around him if he wants to salvage his career

📅 2026-11-25  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Published: 08:21 EST, 25 November 2026 | Updated: 08:23 EST, 25 November 2026

Raheem Sterling wore black to his sentencing hearing today, just as he had when he arrived at Basingstoke Magistrates Court two months ago and admitted to 90 minutes of terrifying rush hour driving, with canisters of nitrous oxide on the passenger seat beside him.

He was visibly more subdued this time, wearing the look of a man who’d spent two months contemplating the prospect of a jail stretch, whilst the contemporaries he once stood with in the England squad, Harry Kane, Jordan Henderson and Jordan Pickford, were continuing to hit the heights.

Imprisonment was only ever a narrow possibility under the sentencing guidelines for dangerous driving, given the miracle that Sterling did not cause serious injury or worse as he zig-zagged between lanes on the M3 and M4 that morning last May. Critically, he was not found to be under the influence of nitrous oxide. A motorist who drove in equally erratic fashion and initially ignored police on Cheshire’s M56, in a BMW which had only three tyres, was handed a suspended sentence earlier this year.

But while the mitigation preceding the magistrate’s decision that Sterling would walk free - his 12-month prison sentence is suspended for 18 months - garlanded him for his charity work and promotion of racial equality, don’t let it be said that his conduct that day was nothing less than a disgrace. And that the tetchy hyper sensitivity Sterling displayed with the police officers who arrested him revealed a shocking lack of contrition and shame.

Sterling has had his challenges, of course. The early death of his father and a nomadic London childhood spent helping his mum on cleaning jobs. But then he disappeared into the cocooned existence of the football academy world, full of the little princes who have people fawning over them, telling them how good they are and throwing obscene amounts of money at them.

Raheem Sterling's conduct the day of his car crash was nothing short of a disgrace

Sterling's meteoric rise has meant he does not have the tools to deal with rejection

Kane has flourished in a way that Sterling hasn’t because he was forced to learn the hard way, deal with rejection and develop a sense of perspective. The princes are at the mercy of the hangers on and leeches, around them for a cut of their next big pay cheque. It was Sterling’s misfortune to select Aidy Ward as his agent – the man who conducted ugly, public contract negotiations with Liverpool which included declaring that Sterling wouldn’t be staying at Anfield even if he were offered nearly £1m a week. The reputational damage that caused Sterling should have seen him sack Ward on the spot.

The problem for those whose adolescence takes place in football’s gilded cage is that they combust and can’t hack it when the going gets remotely tough. For Sterling, read Dele Alli, Kalvin Phillips and Aaron Ramsdale.

But what makes Sterling’s personal disintegration so needless is that he really has had positive influences around him in the background. Friends who were there when he wanted to develop a voice to speak against racial discrimination in football. There when he wanted to establish a Foundation to help the kind of London teenagers that he used to be.

He lost those influences during the chaotic descent into the insanity of that spring morning when he went berserk in his car. Some of them have described to me how he flatly refused to respond to calls and went into ‘oblivion mode’ in the hours after his arrest. It seemed like he was seeking escape from any vestige of the game which is all he has known.

England captain and Sterling's former team-mate Harry Kane has thrived due to the early setbacks in his career

The tragedy is Sterling has a strong support network who want the best for him

The bleak reality for Sterling is that his career in football is surely over now. Yes, he could pick up the thread in some place below the Premier League, though his unhappy experience at Feyenoord – where there was a prurient fascination with his struggles – suggests that he will face the same relentless scrutiny wherever he goes. In the modern world, more gratuitous pleasure than ever is taken in the fall of those who were once great. It will be a circus if he pitches up somewhere new.

But the evidence of that work with boys in London suggests that he has far more of a hinterland to turn to than Alli and his ilk, if only he can re-establish those positive influences and seek help.

A moment in time, precisely two years ago, proves what his future might look like. At a meeting room in King's Cross, Sterling sat with a group of seven young men, all of whom were deemed at risk of drifting into knife crime and who had been sent along to his foundation by Brent's youth offending service.

After an initial 15 or 20 minutes when the boys seemed blinded by the superstar in their midst, they became more comfortable, seeming to feel he understood the likes of them. There were smiles as he described the various school social groups he knew. 'The cool group, the middle of the road group and the geeks,' as he called them. The 'geeks' always seemed to be a bigger part of his world when exams approached, he said with a grin. The room was wreathed with laughter, then.

The group session evolved into several one-to-one conversations between him and the boys who'd been more reticent. It was then that he pointed out the value of allowing outside mentors to help you, if your home life is challenging. 'Having someone else involved changed the course of my life,' Sterling told them.

Now is the moment when Sterling needs others to help change the course of his own life and if he accepts that, he could play a part in public life again. First comes the challenge of rousing himself from any lingering pity, emerging from the celebrity bubble and appreciating that he is supremely lucky to remain a free man after those abominable, mindless few hours in his souped-up car.

Tottenham Open Talks To Complete £8m Transfer, De Zerbi Wants It Done

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Tottenham are already in talks over their first January transfer window deal, according to reports in Turkey.

Spurs have been in a rich vein of form of late, turning their fortunes from the start of the 2026/27 season around with four wins in their last five games to move into the top half of the Premier League table.

Roberto De Zerbi's methods and philosophy are starting to permeate through the playing squad, and while the Italian's teams are normally known for their free-flowing, high-press, attacking football, his Tottenham team is set up with defensive solidity first and foremost.

Tottenham have the second-best Expected Goals Conceded statistics in the top flight so far, only just behind league leaders Arsenal, showing their defensive nous.

It is at the other end of the pitch where they have so far struggled, with just 12 goals in 11 games so far, the joint fourth-worst in the division.

Dominic Solanke has two goals to his name, scoring his second of the campaign on Saturday, with centre-back Jan Paul van Hecke also on two as the club's joint top scorers in the league. Omar Marmoush is the only other recognised striker in the squad, although he can play in a variety of positions across the front line.

That is because Richarlison, the club's top scorer in all competitions in 2025/26, was left out of the 25-man Premier League squad entirely, following a falling out with De Zerbi over his future. De Zerbi initially described Richarlison as having an "unbelievable" attitude, but admitted that he had been left confused by the striker changing his mind over whether or not he wanted to stay at the club.

The Brazilian had been in talks over a move to Everton and Turkish side Trabzonspor in the late stages of the summer transfer window, but a move away did not come to fruition and the 29-year-old is now in the wilderness at Hotspur Way, training and playing with the U21s.

Richarlison only has seven months left on his current deal at Spurs and will be a free agent in the summer. He can also strike up a pre-contract agreement with foreign clubs in the January transfer window, ahead of a move in the summer.

But talks are already underway between Tottenham and Trabzonspor, over a potential move in the January transfer window.

Turkish journalist Yagiz Sabuncuoglu has reported that talks have already taken place between the two clubs and that Spurs are demanding a €10million (£8million).

De Zerbi will be keen to see the Brazilian leave the club in the winter, rather than having someone at the club who is not part of his plans for the second half of the season.

Spurs are also targeting a striker in the winter window, with the Italian keen to add another source of goals to his squad. Eli Junior Kroupi, Igor Mantanovic and Ivan Toney are names who have been linked with a move to N17.

Spurs are looking to extend teengers Luca Williams-Barnett's contract in north London

Only one club has had a better start to their first Champions League season than Como

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Como currently sit in third place in the Champions League table with four victories from their first five games in 2026-27, and only one team has had a better start to their first season in the competition after the Champions League rebrand in 1992.

Como defeated AEK 1-0 at home on Tuesday evening to pick up their fourth win in their first five Champions League matches; Luis Milla providing the only goal of the game.

Cesc Fabregas’s side have also beaten RB Leipzig 4-1 this season, Manchester United 4-1 and Lens 9-1 away from home, the biggest winning margin by an away side in Champions League history. Feyenoord are the only team to have got the better of Como in Europe so far.

Sitting comfortably in third place with 12 points after five matches, and now targeting a top eight and a guaranteed spot in the round of 16, Como have started their first ever season in the Champions League in impressive fashion.

In fact, since 1992, only one team has enjoyed a better start to their first season in the Champions League: That was Leicester City back in 2016-17, off the back of their against-all-odds Premier League victory the season prior.

Under the guidance of Claudio Ranieri, the Foxes won four and drew one of their first five matches in the Champions League, which was enough to secure qualification to the knock-out rounds with one group stage match to spare.

Peter Young is a Senior Reporter with Football Italia.

NFL Week 12 Survivor/Eliminator Picks

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

It's Thanksgiving week, which means a wonky NFL schedule, which means a wonky schedule for articles, which means I'm writing this week's NFL Survivor picks a week earlier than normal. But that doesn't really matter, unless there's something crazy that happens between Wednesday morning and the games Sunday. Let's just say it would have to be really crazy to materially change this week's pick. That brings us to this week's picks. Check out my Week 12 pick for Survivor contests. And while we're here, you can use our NFL Betting Model and our Bet Tracker to make a pick for yourself.

Every week, we have the same process. We eliminate teams in groups until we narrow it down to our real considerations. Start with all 32, whittle it down. And remember — this game is "once you use a team, they're off the board," so it's not always just a matter of taking the team you think will win. There has to be some strategy. I can't pick a team I've picked before, but you might not have tailed me, so previously used teams are eligible until the end. This week, I'm going through the same process, but I'm starting with a different one, simply because it lets me knock so many out at once.

Island games just get weird. I would need some very convincing evidence to pick anyone on a Thursday, or a Monday, or the load of extra games we have this week for the holiday weekend.

Buffalo Bills (vs. Kansas City — Thursday) Carolina Panthers (@ Tampa Bay — Monday) Chicago Bears (@ Detroit — Thursday) Cleveland Browns (vs. Las Vegas — Sunday night) Dallas Cowboys (vs. Philadelphia — Thursday) Denver Broncos (@ Pittsburgh — Friday) Detroit Lions (vs. Chicago — Thursday) Green Bay Packers (@ LA Rams — Wednesday) Kansas City Chiefs (@ Buffalo — Thursday) Las Vegas Raiders (@ Cleveland — Sunday) Los Angeles Rams (vs. Green Bay — Wednesday) Philadelphia Eagles (@ Dallas — Thursday) Pittsburgh Steelers (vs. Denver — Friday) Tampa Bay Buccaneers (vs. Carolina — Monday) That's 14 teams off the board right at the start, which is just so many teams. The NFL is getting greedy, y'all. That leaves us with 18.

Maybe these are good teams, maybe not, but it's too hard to trust them given who they are facing. We are eliminating anyone facing a top-10 team in our FTN NFL Power Rankings. New Orleans Saints (@ Cincinnati) San Francisco 49ers (vs. Seattle) Seattle Seahawks (@ San Francisco) Tennessee Titans (@ Jacksonville) That's only four teams knocked off the list because so many good teams play island games for the holiday, but still. Fourteen to go.

You can face the worst team in all of football, but if you're the second-worst team, well, I'm still not going to be too excited about selecting you. We'll return to the power rankings here and cross off any team in the bottom 12. Arizona Cardinals (vs. Washington) Atlanta Falcons (@ Minnesota) Baltimore Ravens (@ Houston) Indianapolis Colts (vs. NY Giants) Los Angeles Chargers (vs. New England) Miami Dolphins (vs. NY Jets) New England Patriots (@ LA Chargers) New York Jets (@ Miami) The upshot of the NFL putting so many big games in the island spots is that our Sunday has a lot of middling teams facing each other, which means a whopping eight teams get marked off the list here. That leaves us with six teams — our six contenders.

Once we're down to this small a number of teams, we're done cutting them in swaths. We look at the pros and cons for each team and then figure it out. I'm also diving into our NFL Betting Model to see what the numbers think of the game.

Model Projection: Bengals 30.77-21.84, Bengals 70.2% win rate Pros: The Saints have been very disappointing this year, not showing anything like the development many expected from them entering the season. Cons: The Bengals just got kind of shut down by the Commanders; is it possible they aren't as good as we thought?

Cons: "The Texans are getting on track" only applies to one game against the floundering Colts, because it was only a week ago that they lost to the Browns. Trusting the Texans has been cursed this year.

Model Projection: Vikings 20.11-19.78, Vikings 51.0% win rate Pros: Kirk Cousins revenge game! The Vikings defense struggled against the 49ers, but the Falcons are no the 49ers. Cons: Is it that the 49ers are just that good, or did they expose a weakness in the Vikings? And if there's any team that knows how to beat Cousins right now, you'd think it'd be Atlanta.

Inside Raheem Sterling's rise from childhood tragedy and poverty to England superstar at the peak of his powers - but Lamborghini crash and laughing gas addiction leave him staring into the abyss

📅 2026-11-25  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Published: 08:02 EST, 25 November 2026 | Updated: 08:31 EST, 25 November 2026

In September, shortly after pleading guilty to dangerous driving and drug possession, Raheem Sterling shared a statement on social media.

The goal, he implied with a picture of graffiti on a wall, was to 'be better than yesterday' and the intention was to seek 'progress over perfection', as he captioned the image.

But for the former England and Manchester City star to move forward, he has had to fall a long way.

The 31-year-old avoided prison, but was handed a 12-month suspended sentence and 100 hours of community service at Basingstoke Magistrates' Court after he was involved in a dangerous crash in May. Sterling had been driving his £270,000 Lamborghini Urus SUV while under the influence of laughing gas, with multiple motorists spotting him using balloons at the wheel while careening down the M25 and M3 motorways.

During his sentencing hearing, Sterling revealed that he has undergone a 12-day stint in rehab soon after the arrest, after contacting UK addiction services.

The number of footballers in history who have found themselves entangled with the British justice system is not a small one. But against the backdrop of Sterling's personal story, his fall is a particularly poignant one. The former international built a remarkable career out of a difficult upbringing.

Raheem Sterling has been handed a suspended sentence and 100 hours of community service after being convicted of dangerous driving

The former Manchester City star has experienced some of the highest heights in professional football - but now languishes without a club

Sterling lost his father at the age of just two to gun violence, prompting his mother's move from his birthplace of Kingston, Jamaica to the London suburb of Neasden.

Speaking to the Players' Tribune in 2018, Sterling admitted that the tragedy had 'shaped his entire life'.

'For a few years, we lived with our grandmother in Kingston, and I remember watching the other kids with their mums and just feeling really jealous,' he revealed. 'I didn't fully understand what my mum was doing for us. I just knew that she was gone. My grandma was amazing, but everybody wants their mum at that age.'

His father's shocking death also inspired a gun tattoo inked on his leg above his right 'shooting' foot, there as part of a promise he had made that he 'would never touch a gun in his lifetime'. Although the tattoo prompted criticism from anti-gun campaigners in 2018, Sterling was adamant that his message was aligned with theirs, with the player backed by the FA.

After moving to England, his mother Nadine found work as a cleaner. Sterling and his siblings would sometimes help her during her early morning shifts cleaning hotels at 5am before their school day started. With rent a continual challenge for the family as money was tight, they moved house often.

But Sterling's prodigious talent on the football pitch was breaking through, with the winger asked to join Queens Park Rangers' youth set-up at just 10 years old.

Sacrifices were made to allow for this: in 2021, Sterling released a custom football boot with New Balance paying tribute to the three buses - the 18, the 182 and 140 - he had to catch to make the return journey from home near Wembley to the west London training ground, accompanied by his older sister Lakima.

'The red double-deckers with the blue wool '80s vibe on the seats,' he reminisced. 'Spent ages on those. We'd leave at 3.15 and get home at 11 pm. Every. Single. Day.

'She'd sit upstairs in the little cafe and chill until I was done with training. Imagine being 17 years old and doing that for your little brother. And I never once heard her say, 'Nah, I don't wanna take him.'

As a youngster, Sterling's footballing talent was prodigious and helped him sign with QPR at the age of 10

Five years later and Sterling had parlayed this into a move to Liverpool's academy, with the star breaking through to the first team in 2012

'At the time, I didn't understand how much she was sacrificing. Her and my mum got me here. My whole family played a massive part in my life. Without them, you wouldn't even know me.'

Football was a necessary focus as well as passion, with Sterling once removed from mainstream education as a youngster and sent to Vernon House, an adapted school to help with behavioral problems.

His teacher there, Chris Beschi, told the Guardian in 2018 that he had said to a young Sterling: 'If you carry on the way you're going, by the time you're 17 you'll either be playing for England or you'll be in prison.'

But Beschi was able to see a different side to Sterling too, describing the boy as having 'a kind and innocent passion about him', and as football took over, Premier League rivals clamoured to sign him up to their youth ranks.

Nadine was adamant that he should be developed outside of London to avoid any possibility of getting caught up in gang violence, and Chelsea and Arsenal's loss was Liverpool's gain, with Sterling joining the academy at the age of 15 for a then punchy fee of £500,000.

After that, Sterling's career quickly gathered momentum. With the arrival of Liverpool legend Kenny Dalglish in the dugout in early 2011, Sterling was moved into the Europa League squad, fast-tracking integration with established first-team stars. Playing and travel time was limited, however.

Just over a year later, and the player was readied for his first senior start against Wigan Athletic, at the tender age of 17 years and 107 days. The following season saw him named as the club's Young Player of the Year and shortlisted for the PFA Young Player of the Year. By 2014, he was winning the Golden Boy award, and becoming the most expensive English footballer to date with his £49million move to Man City.

But against the backdrop of success on the football pitch, there were challenging moments away from it. In 2013, Sterling was accused of two separate counts of assault, one against a former girlfriend and another against a different woman. He was found not guilty of the former, with the latter incident dropped after a witness failed to attend court.

Two years later, in March 2015, Sterling was snapped by tabloids reportedly smoking from a shisha pipe. And, in a moment that spoke of what was to come, it was alleged that he might have also been using nitrous oxide for recreational purposes. Manager Brendan Rodgers was keen to protect Sterling and urged people to remember: 'Young players make mistakes.'

Sterling had an uneven stint at Liverpool but put on display enough of his talent to earn him the 2014 Golden Boy award

His spell on Merseyside also secured him entrance into the England squad, where he became one of the standouts of the 2010s

Shortly before his move to Man City Sterling started dating Paige Milian, now his long-term partner and mother of his children (Source: Instagram/Raheem Sterling)

The clean slate offered by Man City might have itself been appealing. Things had become somewhat tricky at Anfield, after Sterling had refused a new deal, with the player booed while on duty for England under then-manager Roy Hodgson. Rodgers and Sterling rowed publicly in the media over suggestions that he had turned down a new contract for financial reasons, and his move to the Etihad came after club legends including Steven Gerrard, Jamie Carragher and Graeme Souness had all criticised his conduct.

The first season at the Etihad might have been a false start, with Sterling notably injured in March leading to the premature curtailing of his campaign, but under incoming manager Pep Guardiola, Sterling began flying. At the same time, he was underscoring his importance for England under Hodgson, and then Gareth Southgate.

'In terms of, "Can he fulfil it?", he'll give himself every chance,' Southgate said when asked if he thought Sterling might win the Ballon d'Or after victory against Kosovo in 2019. 'There are some outstanding players around - (Eden) Hazard, (Kevin) De Bruyne, Messi, Ronaldo.

'But he (Sterling) has got the drive, he's got the professionalism, he's got the ability. He's physically and mentally strong for such a small-statured lad.'

And against the backdrop of his growth on the pitch, Sterling was becoming an important voice away from it too, frequently speaking out against the racism young black players were targeted with, and from personal experience. In 2017, Sterling was attacked outside Man City's training ground by a convicted football hooligan hurling racist language at him. In 2018, he suffered racist abuse on the pitch while featuring for Man City against Chelsea.

396 Premier League appearances, 131 goals

GQ magazine described Sterling as football's 'most influential player' in 2019 in recognition of his climb to the very top of the game, and for his increasingly high profile as a campaigner off the pitch.

Sterling branded negative tabloid coverage as racist, and made an address to the United Nations.

'Discrimination is discrimination. It doesn't matter if it's your sexuality, or your color, you shouldn't (discriminate) at all', Sterling said, speaking to the UN Human Rights Office for the International Day for the Elimination of Racial Discrimination. 'We need – as a collective – to take responsibility. This is a new age where color can't define who you are'.

Sterling also established the Raheem Sterling Foundation in 2021, which the footballer funds and which helps to support youth education projects, including fighting knife crime and helping children out of poverty. That year, he was awarded an MBE for services to racial equality in sport.

As his public profile hit new heights, Sterling kept scoring, becoming Man City's seventh-highest goalscorer of all time. But limited game time under Guardiola led to a move to Chelsea for £47.5m, and on a salary worth an eye-watering £325,000 per week.

Sterling was a focal point of the Man City team which achieved a staggering trophy haul in the late 2010s

His move to Chelsea in 2022 set him up to be one of the stars of the club's new era under BlueCo but he failed to hit the right notes across two personally underwhelming seasons

After an abortive loan move to Arsenal, Sterling signed for Feyenoord at the start of the year but did not see his deal extended past the end of the season

In what might be seen as the beginning of the end, his first season in Chelsea blue failed to hit the heights required. There was chaos in west London as the club cycled through Thomas Tuchel, Graham Potter, Bruno Saltor and Frank Lampard in the dugout but Sterling still bagged nine goals and a further four assists in his 38 appearances, as well as securing a place in Southgate's World Cup squad that year.

Sterling made an impact in the group stages in Qatar, but was forced home after his family home was burgled. While his long-term partner Paige Milian and three children were not at the property, Sterling was shaken and flew back to Oxshott from the camp before returning in time for the quarter-final against France.

While his first season with Chelsea had room for improvement at Stamford Bridge, his second was even limper. The latest manager on the club's merry-go-round, Enzo Maresca, said in August 2024 that he was going to 'struggle' for minutes, effectively frozen out.

From there, his on-pitch decline was swift. An abortive loan across the city to Arsenal had the promise of a step up, but Sterling struggled just as hard for game time. A difficult time was compounded by a second break-in at his home, with the family forced to escape but luckily unharmed.

On his return to Chelsea, Maresca placed him in the now-infamous bomb squad, forced to train away from the first-team players, share separate, smaller locker rooms, and all but ostracised within Cobham.

Offers swirled around Sterling as they have done for all his decorated career, but the possibility of a move to Bayer Leverkusen or Juventus did not go much further than exploratory talks. A switch to nearby Fulham fell through. With money far from a motivating factor, the winger opted not to accept a lucrative contract and move to Saudi Arabia.

Instead, Sterling stayed out of the game until his contract was mutually terminated in January of this year.

Hopes of finding that at his next club Feyenoord evaporated on his debut. Signed until the end of the 2025-26 season, Sterling stepped onto the pitch in February after nine months out and looked rusty.

The critics were brutal, but head coach Robin van Persie did his best to defend his new player. It was too harsh to judge him over just six or eight weeks, he stressed.

Within weeks, he was back in the UK, with his arrest coming in May. Any offers that might have come his way over the summer have subsequently dried up.

Sterling crashed his £270,000 Lambourghini Urus while driving under the influence

The arrest took place shortly after his time in Rotterdam came to an end, with the star pleading guilty in September

Since the arrest, Sterling has been keeping a low profile and focusing on his family (Source: Instagram/Raheem Sterling)

With offers from football clubs quieter, the winger has focused on other ventures including his Playmaker Films (Source: Instagram/Raheem Sterling)

Sterling has since worked on his Playmaker Films, a content agency, and launched 'The Cage', a grassroots football competition where teams of four face off for eight minutes with £5,000 on the line.

'Football's about expressing yourself,' he said when interviewed about why he created the contest, which showcases individuality on caged pitches similar to ones he would have learned to play on. 'The game is a beautiful thing'.

Sterling's story is of a remarkable rise from adversity to the highest levels in professional sport. His private life has clearly been complicated - District Judge Kirsty Allman revealed his drug problem had 'become a maladaptive coping mechanism during a difficult period in his career'.

Why ‘full package’ Jude Bellingham is the best player in the world right now

📅 Nov 25, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

It says something if your compatriot wins the Ballon d’Or and you still get named your country’s player of the year ahead of him.

Jude Bellingham didn’t have a strong enough 2025-26 season with Real Madrid to really be in contention for the prize that Harry Kane won, but he did have a better World Cup than Kane, leading to the retention of his England player of the year accolade – and it seems to have been the springboard for better things at club level.

Last season, he scored eight goals and made five assists for Real Madrid. This season, he’s just moved on to seven goals and five assists. By the way, it’s November.

In other words, this is a player back to his best. But it’s not just about his productivity. He’s doing things with a football that few others can.

Take, for example, his goal for Madrid against PSV in the Champions League on Tuesday night. It’s one of those goals that looks good in the first place and yet still gets better with every replay and each new angle.

He collects the ball after a lay-off by Kylian Mbappe, flicks it up with his second touch and then sends it flying into the top corner with a swerving volley* across the outside of his foot.

Bellingham gives the hosts the lead with a magnificent volley 🔥

📺 Watch live on TNT Sports & HBO Max https://t.co/fRCRh6tXYB pic.twitter.com/WdP6ULx8wE

— Football on TNT Sports (@footballontnt) November 24, 2026

* Some will say it’s not a volley in the strictest sense, but who cares? Just sit back and enjoy.

Would it be an exaggeration to say that could be a Puskas Award contender? It’s got to be worthy of a shout.

It’s a goal that demonstrates the confidence flowing through the veins of an elite talent. He’s playing with a nonchalance befitting of one of the best players in the world.

And what really ensures Bellingham is in that category is the fact that he’s the full package. He plays in a position that demands you can do it all – and he has all the answers.

Bellingham plays with purpose and direction. As an attacking midfielder, he can create chances and convert them in equal measure. And sometimes it’s spectacular.

How many players in the world are on top of their game like Bellingham right now? Only a few are in the same conversation.

If he can keep this up for the course of the season and produce a few more moments of magic, we might be looking at an English Ballon d’Or contender for the second year in a row.
